definition{
  name: budget,
  parameters: [],
  result: Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Budget,
  body: (record Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Budget {maxWork := (100000000 : UInt64), maxOwnedBytes := (1000000000 : UInt64), maxOutputBytes := (100000000 : UInt64), maxFrames := (100 : UInt64)}),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: drive,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, state : Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State],
  result: (Result Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State ScenarioError),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fuel))
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (project state disposition)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.running => (drive rest (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::tick state))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cancelling => (drive rest (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::tick state))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.suspended reason => (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.suspended reason))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.failed failure => (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.engine failure))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.completed output => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cleaned => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: observeByte,
  parameters: [
    fuel : Nat,
    arena : Uorc.Core.Heap::Arena,
    root : Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle,
    index : UInt64
  ],
  result: (Result UInt8 ScenarioError),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fuel))
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (Uorc.Core.Heap::lookup arena root)
      | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Result.ok entry => (match (project entry node)
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.sequenceLeaf image offset count => (match (Uorc.Core.RuntimeSequence::readByte arena image (Uorc.Core.Heap::wordAdd offset index))
          | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
          | Result.ok byte => (ctor Result.ok [UInt8, ScenarioError] byte))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.sequenceBranch length height left right => (match (Uorc.Core.Heap::sequenceInfo arena left)
          | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
          | Result.ok info => (if (Uorc.Core.Heap::less index (project info length)) then (observeByte rest arena left index) else (observeByte rest arena right (Uorc.Core.Heap::wordSub index (project info length)))))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.image kind bytes => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.storeLeaf byte => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.storeBranch left right => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.slot role value => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.rootBranch role left right => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.metadataWord kind key value => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.metadataLink kind key target => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.metadataBranch kind left right => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.ownerRoot role target => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
        | Uorc.Core.Heap::Node.imageReservation kind length => (ctor Result.error [UInt8, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: compareBytes,
  parameters: [
    remaining : Nat,
    arena : Uorc.Core.Heap::Arena,
    root : Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle,
    expected : Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Sequence,
    index : UInt64
  ],
  result: (Result Unit ScenarioError),
  body: (match remaining
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Result.ok [Unit, ScenarioError] ())
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (apply (project expected read) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeSequence::wordToNatAux 64 index))
      | Option.none => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Option.some wanted => (match (observeByte 500 arena root index)
        | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
        | Result.ok got => (match (prim checked_convert got : (Option UInt64))
          | Option.none => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
          | Option.some wide => (if (prim equal (Uorc.Core.RuntimeSequence::wordToNatAux 64 wide) wanted : Bool) then (compareBytes rest arena root expected (Uorc.Core.Heap::wordAdd index (1 : UInt64))) else (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false,
  recursive_argument: remaining
}

definition{
  name: checkOutput,
  parameters: [
    state : Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State,
    expected : Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Sequence
  ],
  result: (Result Unit ScenarioError),
  body: (if (Uorc.Core.HeapAudit::auditRoots (project state arena) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::ownedRoots state)) then (match (project state disposition)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.completed output => (match (Uorc.Core.RuntimeSequence::length (project state arena) output)
      | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Result.ok count => (if (prim equal (Uorc.Core.RuntimeSequence::wordToNatAux 64 count) (project expected length) : Bool) then (compareBytes (project expected length) (project state arena) output expected (0 : UInt64)) else (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))))
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.failed failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.engine failure))
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.suspended reason => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.suspended reason))
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.running => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fuel))
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cancelling => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fuel))
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cleaned => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))) else (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.audit (1 : UInt64)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: startFixture,
  parameters: [
    specs : (List Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Block),
    entry : UInt64,
    basis : (List Bytes)
  ],
  result: (Result Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State ScenarioError),
  body: (match (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::ready specs entry basis)
    | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fixture failure))
    | Result.ok ready => (if (Uorc.Core.HeapAudit::auditRoots (project ready arena) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::rootInventory (project ready program) (project ready caller))) then (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::start ready (budget))) else (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.audit (0 : UInt64))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: execute,
  parameters: [
    specs : (List Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Block),
    entry : UInt64,
    basis : (List Bytes),
    referenceGraph : Uorc.Core.Graph::Graph,
    referenceBasis : (List Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Sequence)
  ],
  result: (Result Unit ScenarioError),
  body: (match (Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::evaluateGraph referenceGraph referenceBasis)
    | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.reference failure))
    | Result.ok expected => (match expected
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Value.sequence sequence => (match (startFixture specs entry basis)
        | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
        | Result.ok initial => (match (drive 300000 initial)
          | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
          | Result.ok finished => (checkOutput finished sequence)))
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Value.natural value => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Value.byte value => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Value.predicate value => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Value.store value => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: settle,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, state : Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State],
  result: (Result Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State ScenarioError),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Result.error [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fuel))
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (project state disposition)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.running => (settle rest (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::tick state))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cancelling => (settle rest (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::tick state))
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.suspended reason => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.failed failure => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.completed output => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state)
      | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cleaned => (ctor Result.ok [Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::State, ScenarioError] state))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false,
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: executeSemanticFailure,
  parameters: [
    specs : (List Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Block),
    entry : UInt64,
    referenceGraph : Uorc.Core.Graph::Graph
  ],
  result: (Result Unit ScenarioError),
  body: (match (Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::evaluateGraph referenceGraph (nil Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Sequence))
    | Result.ok unexpected => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
    | Result.error referenceError => (match referenceError
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Failure.semantic expectedError => (match (startFixture specs entry (nil Bytes))
        | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
        | Result.ok initial => (match (settle 300000 initial)
          | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
          | Result.ok finished => (if (Uorc.Core.HeapAudit::auditRoots (project finished arena) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::ownedRoots finished)) then (match (project finished disposition)
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.failed error => (match error
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.semantic semantic => (if (prim equal (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineTests::semanticCode semantic) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineTests::referenceSemanticCode expectedError) : Bool) then (ctor Result.ok [Unit, ScenarioError] ()) else (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch)))
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.program x => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.resource x => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.heap x => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.cancelled => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
              | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Failure.invariant => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch)))
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.running => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cancelling => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.completed x => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.cleaned => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))
            | Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachine::Disposition.suspended x => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch))) else (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.audit (2 : UInt64))))))
      | Uorc.Core.ReferenceSemantics::Failure.static staticError => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.mismatch)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: resourceCode,
  parameters: [reason : Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error],
  result: UInt64,
  body: (match reason
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.workLimit => (0 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.frameLimit => (1 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.outputLimit => (2 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.ownedByteLimit => (3 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.addressSpaceExhausted => (9 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.invalidUsage => (9 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.arenaBudgetMismatch => (9 : UInt64)
    | Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Error.heap error => (9 : UInt64)),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: false
}

definition{
  name: workPauseExtend,
  parameters: [],
  result: (Result Unit ScenarioError),
  body: (match (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::ready (cons (record Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Block {parameters := (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::RuntimeType), resultType := (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::RuntimeType.sequence), instructions := (cons (record Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Instruction {opcode := (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeOpcode::Opcode.sequenceConstant), operand0 := (0 : UInt64), operand1 := (0 : UInt64), operand2 := (0 : UInt64), operand3 := (0 : UInt64), capturesA := (nil UInt64), capturesB := (nil UInt64), literal := 0x010203}) (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Instruction)), returnDistance := (1 : UInt64), initialRelease := (nil UInt64), releaseAfter := (nil (List UInt64))}) (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineFixtures::Block)) (0 : UInt64) (nil Bytes))
    | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] (ctor ScenarioError.fixture failure))
    | Result.ok ready => (let limit : UInt64 := (1000000000 : UInt64); (match (settle 300000 (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::start (record Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Ready {arena := (record Uorc.Core.Heap::Arena {index := (project (project ready arena) index), nextHandle := (project (project ready arena) nextHandle), exhausted := (project (project ready arena) exhausted), ownedBytes := (project (project ready arena) ownedBytes), liveEntries := (project (project ready arena) liveEntries), maxOwnedBytes := limit, cursor := (project (project ready arena) cursor)}), program := (project ready program), caller := (project ready caller), usage := (project ready usage)}) (record Uorc.Core.RuntimeResources::Budget {maxWork := (0 : UInt64), maxOwnedBytes := limit, maxOutputBytes := (100000000 : UInt64), maxFrames := (100 : UInt64)})))
      | Result.error failure => (ctor Result.error [Unit, ScenarioError] failure)
      | Result.ok paused => (if (Uorc.Core.HeapAudit::auditRoots (project paused arena) (Uorc.Core.RuntimeMachineEngine::ownedRoots paused)) then (match (project paused disposition)
